<input type='checkbox' name='article_1363' value='https://thehackernews.com/2025/11/ctm360-exposes-global-whatsapp.html' /> 1363 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=thehackernews.com+CTM360+Exposes+a+Global+WhatsApp+Hijacking+Campaign%3A+HackOnChat%EE%A0%82Nov+20%2C+2025Online+Fraud+%2F+Web+SecurityCTM360++has+identified+a+rapidly+expanding+WhatsApp+account-hacking+campaign+targeting+users+worldwide+via+a+network+of+deceptive+authentication+portals+and+impersonation+pages.+The+campaign%2C+internally+dubbed+HackOnChat%2C+abuses+WhatsApp%27s+familiar+web+interface%2C+using+social+engineering+tactics+to+trick+users+into+compromising+their+accounts.++Investigators+identified+thousands+of+malicious+URLs+being+hosted+on+inexpensive+top-level+domains+and+rapidly+generated+through+modern+website-building+platforms%2C+allowing+attackers+to+deploy+new+pages+at+scale.+The+campaign%27s+activity+logs+show+hundreds+of+incidents+in+recent+weeks%2C+with+a+noticeable+surge+across+the+Middle+East+and+Asia.++Read+the+full+report+here%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fwww.ctm360.com%2Freports%2Fhackonchat-unmasking-the-whatsapp-hacking-scam++The+hacking+operations+and+the+exploitation+techniques++Two+techniques+dominate+these+hacking+operations.+The+Session+Hijacking+%2C+where+threat+actors+misuse+the+linked-device+functionality+to+hijack+act...' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://thehackernews.com/2025/11/ctm360-exposes-global-whatsapp.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CTM360 Exposes a Global WhatsApp Hijacking Campaign: HackOnChatNov 20, 2025Online Fraud / Web SecurityCTM360  has identified a rapidly expanding WhatsApp account-hacking campaign targeting users worldwide via a network of deceptive authentication portals and impersonation pages. The campaign, internally dubbed HackOnChat, abuses WhatsApp's familiar web interface, using social engineering tactics to trick users into compromising their accounts.  Investigators identified thousands of malicious URLs being hosted on inexpensive top-level domains and rapidly generated through modern website-building platforms, allowing attackers to deploy new pages at scale.
